Theorem 215.1001 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on general_chemistry structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 565. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 271 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the general_chemistry decomposition. By minimality, the element a^565 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 271-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
